Deleted with it: **`.nav-top-shadow`** — the bare transparent `position: fixed` sibling of `q-footer` that traced the bar at z 3040 to throw a ~9px cast upward (it existed only because a `box-shadow` written on `.nav-bar` was buried by the footer's own 2600 stacking context) — and the `--shadow-side-edge` casts on both rail slots. The bar's `border-top` states its edge alone now. The **body of the bar stays the pale brown-1 plaque** — the two rail slots at its ends are the only sections painted brown-4. **Layout since 2026-08-02** — Left: the **burger slot**, alone — and only while the drawer is hidden (it lives in the drawer's own footer block otherwise; see the mirror-twin note below). Center: **nothing**. Right cluster: `GlobalSearch` · **chat** button (icon only) · hairline · **create bundle** (`q-btn-group`: make post + skeleton builder + label maker + upload) · hairline · the **pin tack in its slot**. Three things were removed that day, at the user's ask: (1) the left cluster's **profile** and **logout** `nav-btn`s — both moved into the left drawer, whose profile row is now a dense identity block and where an action belongs with the identity it ends; (2) the ENTIRE `.nav-center` — back/forward, the checkpoint trio, the step + current-title readout and the checkpoint dropdown, with their styles (`.checkpoint-btn`, `.nav-path-info`, `.step-indicator`, `.checkpoint-indicator`, `.nav-divider-v`, `.current-title`, `.checkpoint-list*`), so `useNavStore`/`useAuthStore`/`typeIcon` are no longer read here — navigation history is reached through the drawer's Back button and the stack widget, and the current element names itself on the page; (3) the chat button's **"chat" label** (`.chat-btn-label`), the glyph and tooltip saying it already. `.tack-slot` is the **parked pinned column continued down through the bar**: `calc(var(--dock-rail-w) - 1px)` (41px) wide with `margin-left: -6px` cancelling `.nav-right`'s gap (the two track each other) so it butts against the hairline, `align-self: stretch` + flex-centred. Hairline + slot = exactly `var(--dock-rail-w)` (42px) flush at the screen edge, so that hairline lands on the **same pixel as the parked column's own brown-4 left border** and the two read as one uninterrupted right-edge column. **The slot wears that column's coat, not the bar's** (2026-07-24): a solid **`--brown-4`** background running the bar's full height (the brown-4 hairline before it melts into it, so the column simply *continues* through the footer). It is **FLAT since 2026-08-02** — it wore the shared `--shadow-side-edge` cast leftward *on top of* the brown-1 plaque (with `position: relative; z-index: 1` to keep it above the bar and the bundle beside it), which is ink painted on the bar exactly where the block meets it: it darkened the plaque AND made the 41px block read ~5px wider than the 42px column above it. **THE TACK WEARS `NodeMini`'s ROUND PILL SINCE 2026-08-22** (user ask: take "the rounded one with the node icon inside" from the node viewer's top-left corner and "use it on the bottom right's pin button", "instead of teal-13, use red-13"). `.node-mini__round-pill`'s material arrives whole — **`--grey-3`** fill, a **`--grey-5`** hairline RING outside the box (`0 0 0 1px`) and SEAM inside its bottom edge (`inset 0 -1px 0`), both box-shadows because the one border is spent on the base and a shadow follows `border-radius` where an `outline` may not, a 2px coloured BASE curving along the bottom arc, **`--grey-8`** ink with the `-webkit-text-stroke: 0.35px` that thickens a small glyph without enlarging it. **ONE line differs: the base is `--red-13` (`#ff1744`) where the pill's is `--teal-12`** — the whole distance between the two objects, same material and different thing. The DIAMETER stays 24px, not the pill's 18px (the slot was built around 24; 18 is a fact about a header row, not about the material), and hover lifts the pale face to **`--grey-1`** rather than touching the base, since --teal-12/-13's rule is that an object's own mark and the pointer's are never the same colour at once. Verified by `flow-tack-pill.mjs`: `--red-13` resolves as a real token (the CSS carries literal fallbacks that would otherwise hide a missing one) and both copies read identically, resting and pinned, at 1440px and at 375px. Inside its slot the tack is a **24px circle with no inner padding** (`border-radius: 50%`, 13px `push_pin` — 28px/15px until the bar shortened on 2026-08-02, when 28px stopped leaving air in a 31px content box — the *same box and icon size as the parked column's chips*, so it reads as one of them) floating centred with ~6.5px of breathing room each side; the earlier 41px-wide stadium slab is gone (2026-07-24). Its skin was the **drawer's Back button, not Quasar's push preset** (`round unelevated`, `push` dropped) until 2026-08-22, and the BURGER still is: a flat **inverted brown chip** — solid **brown-8** face (`--brown-8`, `#4e342e`) with a *matching* brown-8 rim so there is no contrasting outline, a **brown-1** glyph (`--brown-1`), a hairline `inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.25)` highlight along the top, and a Quasar brown-7 `#5d4037` lift on hover (the old grey-1/pink-13 push pebble is gone). The tack's own hover is the mirror of that rule — a pale chip lifts by going PALER, to `--grey-1`. When the current element **is pinned** (`.is-pinned`) the chip's **BASE CLOSES INTO A RING** (2026-08-22): `border: 2px solid var(--red-13)` all the way round, the pushpin taking the same red as ink, and the grey hairlines standing down (`box-shadow: none`) so the rim is not drawn twice. **The state changed language with the skin** — it used to INVERT, a dark face going pale (**brown-1** face + **brown-8** outline and pushpin; brown-4 for those two until 2026-07-24, when painting the slot brown-4 swallowed them whole), which is a statement in VALUE and impossible for a pill that is pale in both states; so the state moved onto the MARK instead — matching the dock plaque chrome (the side panels' own outer borders keep brown-4 — they were briefly lightened to brown-3 on 2026-07-24, then put back the same day). The tack sits **last** so it anchors the corner right beneath the pinned side panel and shares its exact column; there is **no separate pinned-list toggle** anymore. **THE TACK BELONGS TO THE PINNED COLUMN NOW, AND THIS ONE IS ITS FALLBACK** (2026-08-02, user ask: "make sure the pin button is contained inside [the pinned side bar] and that the sidebar is on top of the footer") — `PinsDrawer` runs to the window floor over this bar's right end and rebuilds the bar's row inside itself around the tack, so the bar renders its own copy only when that column is ABSENT: **`showTack = windows.isMobile || !windows.panels.pins.open`**, the same hand-off `showBurger` gives the burger at the other end, with the preceding `.nav-divider` `v-if`'d alongside it. Mobile is the live case — ≤600px hides both side widgets and pinning has to stay reachable there (verified at 375px: the slot renders at x 334…375 and the create bundle ends exactly at 334). Neither the pins nor the navigation-stack panel has a button here — both dock flush to the screen edge and expand on hover over their parked icon column (the head glyph taps for touch). **THE BURGER IS THE TACK'S MIRROR TWIN** (2026-08-02): `.burger-slot` and `.burger-btn` are built from the tack's own rules — the selectors `.burger-slot, .tack-slot` and `.nav-bar .tack-btn, .nav-bar .burger-btn` were literally shared — so the left end gets the same `calc(var(--dock-rail-w) - 1px)` brown-4 block running the bar's full height, the same 24px circle floating centred in it, and (since both went flat that same day) no cast at all. The result is a window bounded by two matching vertical strips: the drawer's 42px mini rail continued down through the bar on the left, the parked pinned column continued down on the right. **The two left edges line up to the pixel, and the number is FETCHED rather than typed**: the block is `calc(var(--dock-rail-w) - 1px)` = 41px and `.nav-left`'s `border-right` completes it to 42, while MainLayout's `q-drawer` takes `:mini-width="drawerRailW"` — read once in `setup()` off the `--dock-rail-w` custom property (fallback 42) instead of the hard-coded `42` it used to carry, so the drawer rail and the burger block cannot drift (a mismatch shows as a step at the bottom-left corner). Verified: `aside.q-drawer` and `.nav-left` both end at `x = 42`. **THE BAR ONLY OWNS THAT BLOCK WHILE THERE IS NO DRAWER** (later the same day, user ask): the drawer now runs to the window floor and lies over this bar, carrying the burger in its own `.drawer-footer` block on exactly these pixels — so `.burger-slot` is `v-if="showBurger"` (MainLayout: `!drawer && !hideDrawer`, i.e. never both, and no dead button on a `hideDrawer` route), and `.nav-left` takes `.nav-left--bare` in that state, dropping its `border-right` too: a lone brown-3 line at `x = 0` under the drawer's shadow reads as a seam in a surface meant to be continuous. The section keeps its grid track either way — the template is `auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto` and removing a cell would re-map the other two. The chip is the OPEN half of the pair (`@toggle-drawer` → MainLayout's `drawer = !drawer`; the drawer's own chip is the close half), so its glyph is fixed: `menu` **rotated 90°** (`.burger-glyph--upright`, riding the `transform` transition the shared chip rule already runs to tip the pushpin) — one burger stood on end, not a second icon (`view_week`'s three vertical bars were tried first and read as a pause button), answering the `menu_open` the drawer's chip wears. The `drawerOpen` prop is gone with the conditional glyph. It deliberately does **not** invert on state the way the tack does: the tack's pale face states a fact about the page that nothing else on screen carries, whereas the drawer states its own presence, and a permanently pale left chip would break the twinning. **MOBILE** (`@media (max-width: 600px)` in this component's scoped block, in step with `_components.scss` and `MOBILE_MQ`): both rail blocks hold their full 42px — at 375px they are the ONLY way to reach the drawer and to pin the page, the stack/pins side widgets being hidden — and the right cluster compresses around them instead: gaps `8px → 4px`, both `.nav-divider`s dropped (the cluster is short enough to read as one group without them), `.nav-btn` and the create bundle's buttons to `min-width: 30px` / `0 5px` padding, `.tack-slot`'s negative margin retuned to the new gap, minitab labels capped at 9ch. Measured at 375px: no two controls overlap and ~90px of slack remains. Also renders the **minitab strip** — the minimized maker/uploader/builders/chat park as folder tabs on the bar's top edge (2026-07-27; they stood ON the frieze footer band while it occupied that line, until 2026-08-04): a lifted `position: fixed` SIBLING of the q-footer at `bottom: var(--nav-footer-h)`, z **3045**, each tab's bottom seated exactly on the bar's top edge — and **casting nothing** (2026-08-02, user ask: an open maker/uploader tab "looks like it's floating on top of the bar"). It wore `0 2px 8px` onto the band it stands over; that DOWNWARD reach, landing at the bar's own top edge, is what made a parked tab hover instead of attach. A folder tab is stuck to its drawer, and the shared brown-1 face plus the border say so. The strip stays a lifted sibling because the footer was a z-2600 stacking context and the band was opaque at 3000, so nothing inside the footer could paint over it (the footer is 3110 now and the band is retired, but the strip's own 3045 is what keeps parked tabs clickable over every expanded dock (3010+) while staying under the drawer (3050)). (From 2026-07-25 until then the strip sat INSIDE the footer on the band's top edge — `bottom: calc(100% + var(--frieze-h))`; before that on the bar's own top edge until that opaque band moved in, which would have swallowed every parked tab whole) (teal edit_note = maker, purple upload = uploader, slate architecture = schema builder); the stack/pins side bars **narrow to thin icon columns** on the right edge instead (each is ONE element with two presentations, `is-parked` ⇄ expanded panel at the same screen edge — 2026-07-24 third pass, the separate rail tree is gone). `.nav-bar` is exactly `var(--nav-bar-h)` (**32px** since 2026-08-02) tall — **its own ROW, not the whole footer**: since 2026-08-02 a **FLOOR FRIEZE BAND** stands under it inside the same `q-footer` (user ask, "a dark brown frieze bar below the nav footer bar, the ones from inside the left drawer") — and since **2026-08-04 it wears the crown strip's own PALE palette** (`.nav-floor-frieze`: `--frieze-bar-base` brown-1 + `--frieze-bar-wave-{one,two}` brown-3/brown-4, the same dial seam the chat dock's green band uses; it wore the drawer's dark brown-4/-2/-1 recipe for its first two days, when `FriezeFooter` riding this bar's top edge was the pale mirror and the page was closed by two bands — that band is deleted and **this is the page's one closing band now**). It is mounted INSIDE the footer rather than as a fixed sibling because the footer is what Quasar measures to pad the page, so the page's bottom inset grows with it and nothing can slide beneath the strip. `--nav-footer-h` is therefore the TOTAL (`calc(var(--nav-bar-h) + var(--frieze-h))`) and keeps its exact old MEANING — how far up from the window floor the chrome reaches, still this bar's TOP edge — so **nothing anchored to it needed touching**; only `.nav-bar` itself, which wants the row. The drawer and the pinned column run PAST this footer to the window floor and so repeated the band themselves (`.drawer-floor-frieze`, `.pins-floor-frieze`), making the line unbroken edge to edge. ⚠ **ALL THREE ARE GONE SINCE 2026-08-12** (user ask) — the footer is the bar's row alone and `--nav-footer-h` is plain `--nav-bar-h`. The **docks and the minitab strip**, which sit at `bottom: var(--nav-footer-h)`, merge with its top edge — the pinned column and the left drawer no longer stop there at all, both running past it to `bottom: 0`, owning the floor band's pixels at the two ends and repeating it themselves (`.drawer-floor-frieze`, `.pins-floor-frieze`) so the line ran unbroken — until all three left on 2026-08-12. (`--dock-bottom`, the token that once stood bottom docks one `--frieze-h` higher on the retired `FriezeFooter` band, is gone since 2026-08-04.) The pins widget stood on the bar's top edge (`bottom: var(--nav-footer-h)`) in **both** presentations until 2026-08-02; it runs to `bottom: 0` at z 3120 now, lying OVER the bar's last 42px and carrying the tack itself, and the bar reserves that width (`.nav-right--railed`) so it still covers no buttons. The **minitab strip** shares that band, so it shifts left by `windows.footerPanelInset` (`PANEL_W` when the pins panel is expanded at the screen edge, `RAIL_W` when parked) so parked maker/uploader tabs stay clear of the pins column. **DASHBOARD BUTTON (2026-08-10, user ask)** — a `sym_o_empty_dashboard` pebble in its OWN section of the right cluster, hairlined on both sides, between the chat button and the blue create bundle: chat is where entities meet, the bundle is where elements are made, and this opens a box that only LOOKS at the platform, so it is a third thing rather than a pale member of either group. **The one GREY control on this bar** — a `--grey-3` face inside a `--grey-4` rim with `--grey-9` ink, which is exactly the plaque-and-border pair of the flyout it opens (active/hover step the face to `--grey-4` and the rim to `--grey-6`: the pressed state of a grey object is a darker grey, not an accent). It reads `stores/dashboard.js` STRAIGHT, exactly as the chat button reads its own store, and toggles in the same three steps (closed → open, parked → restore, standing → close). Its flyout pass took a `dashboardOpen` prop and emitted `toggle-dashboard`; both are gone — the panel became a dock that rises from this bar and parks on it, so the bar is one of the two surfaces that OWN it and has no business being told about it from above. **Its minitab lives in `parkedTabs` beside chat's** (2026-08-10, user ask: "for the dashboard, make it a tab on the bottom nav footer bar") — the strip was already the parking lot for every window rising from this bar, so the destination cost one entry and a tint, `.minitab__icon--dashboard` at `#424242`, the only grey mark in a row of teal/violet/slate ones and the same ink the pebble four pixels above it letters its glyph in. ⚠ `sym_o_` prefixed: `empty_dashboard` exists only in Material SYMBOLS and an unmatched ligature renders as the literal word (specs/gotchas.md). Every palette paragraph above describing a cream motif is history (the grey-8 plate survives — it is still the trio's); wave ONE's dial left with the layer slim v-ifs out, and the fit/carve dials reach through `slim` since the same pass (see the FriezeBar row). Geometry untouched: 21px trail, chips riding inside it, `auto 13px`, carve off. ⭐ **AND THE SITTING'S LAST ASK SWAPPED THE TWO BANDS** ("invert the frieze pattern on the top nav bar with the bottom footer nav bar one"): the ramp went back upstairs and this trail wears the header's recolor now — plate = **the bar's own `--plaque-coat`** (legal in the dial because FriezeBar spends `--frieze-bar-base` in a `background:` shorthand; band and bar composite to the same rgb, so the band VANISHES and the buttons ride a bare meander), wave = **flat `--grey-6`**, no `-paint`. ⚠ The trail LEFT the trio's grey-8 plate, and the band is VALUE-INVERTED again (its second time — the cream hour was the first), so the carve is forbidden by the lighter-ink law again, not just the trio's grime reason. ⭐ **AND THE DAY'S LAST ASKS RETURNED THE DRAWER'S PATTERN** ("changing the svg-pattern to the one on the inner friezebars from the left drawer, with the 2 svgs well drawn instead of the single tiny one", then "invert the colors on each svg and use grey-7 instead of grey-8"): `slim` OFF the mount — the route the component note always named for the second depth's return — so BOTH waves draw, **wave one `--grey-6`, wave two `--grey-7`** (grey-8/grey-6 held one pass; the settled pair is one step apart, lighter ink on the thick wave), still on the coat plate. ⭐ **THEN THE THICKENING ASK** ("make the pattern slightly thicker by reducing padding and size of outer components from the frieze bar"): `--frieze-bar-pad: 0` (layer 19px → the band's full 21px) + **fit `auto 13px` → `auto 17px`** — ~1.31px a motif row, knowingly off the trio's pixel grid (an upscale antialiases stroke edges — soft, not the squeeze-era mush; crisp rungs remain 13n), and the band itself unmoved at 21px because `--nav-trail-h` is also every chip's height. | `layout/StackPanel.vue` | The navigation stack — a docked right side widget that hangs from the screen's **very top-right corner** (`top: 0` from 2026-07-24 — **over** the crown strip, which its z 3100 already outranked; it sat below the strip at `top: var(--frieze-h)` for part of that day; and it is back on **`top: 0`** since 2026-08-18 (user ask: this column and the left drawer both start at the very top, with the silver rail drawing on top of both — it had stepped down by `--media-tabs-h` from 2026-08-05, and now the rail OVERLAPS its frieze band instead of standing above it) and grows downward toward the middle (mirror of `PinsDrawer`), wearing **TWO mirrored `FriezeBar` bands that BRACKET its scroll well** (`flex-shrink: 0`, both `--frieze-bar-h` = 0.96 × `--frieze-h` ≈ 18.1px @900 since 2026-08-17's trim walk — full `--frieze-h` 18.9px before it, 27px before the 2026-08-02 thinning — both rendered in **both presentations** — at rail width they show a small wave slice; **both carry `flip` since 2026-08-02**, so this column's meander runs opposite the left drawer's — see the FriezeBar row): `.stack-frieze` at the widget's own **top edge** and `.stack-frieze--inner` between the **well and the header/head glyph** at the bottom (2026-07-24 — the mirror of the one band `PinsDrawer` carries). Only the top band **stands in for** the crown strip across the widget's column rather than dividing the list from it, so it wears `FriezeHeader`'s **exact box**: `height: var(--frieze-h)` + a 1px bottom lip (`var(--grey-6)` since 2026-08-17's rim ask, the strip's own `var(--brown-3)` before), which (border-box) trims the same 1px off the carve area so both motifs draw at an identical size and the band doesn't step at the widget's left edge; the widget's own **`border-top` is dropped** for the same reason — the band, not a brown-4 hairline, is what meets the screen edge (verified: band and strip both measure `top 0, h 27, border-bottom 1px, padding-top 1px`, parked AND expanded). Only the palette differs by design — since 2026-08-17 (user ask) it comes from the **side-chrome trio** rule in `_components.scss` — three paints and nothing else since 2026-08-21's from-scratch asks: `--grey-8` plate, `--grey-2` thick wave, `--grey-4` other wave, carve off, pixel-drawn at `15px`/`auto 13px` (an orange↔teal gradient weave rimmed `--grey-6` 08-17 → 08-21; FriezeBar's inverted brown-4 base until then) — shared with `.pins-frieze` and the drawer's `.drawer-frieze` bands. ⚠ The crown lip is GONE with the trio's rims (2026-08-21): brown-3 from birth, `--grey-6` after the rim ask, ended by "three paints" — do not re-add it without the rims or this one band steps at the widget's left edge. The **inner** band takes no lip (plain `height: var(--frieze-h)`) — it stands in for nothing, so border-box resolves it to the very same 27px box and the two read as a matched pair; both run **full bleed** across the widget while the well between them keeps its margins. **ONE element with two presentations** (2026-07-24, third pass — the separate rail tree of the second pass is gone): expanded = the 300px panel, parked (`is-parked`, when the window is minimized) = the **same element narrowed to a thin icon column** (`--dock-rail-w`, 42px, shrink-to-fit height); the list is the **one and only item scroller** — the same DOM node in both presentations, so the **scroll position carries through the park/expand morph** (carried as a ratio — the item faces differ in height), and the shared `.dock-window` width transition animates it. The `.dock-bar` **info box** sits at the **bottom** of the expanded panel (below the list): layers icon + title left-aligned and **both in `--brown-8`** (2026-07-24, 8th pass — one consistent chrome ink; the old `.dock-bar__icon--stack` ink-2 tint is deleted), the step count meta in the lighter **`--brown-4`** at the right, and a **`.dock-bar__action` history button** (flat brown-8 `history` clock glyph, tooltip "Navigation stack history") at the very right end — `@click.stop` so it doesn't park the widget through the bar; **its destination landed 2026-07-31 (Thread H)**: it routes to the user's NAVIGATION skeleton viewer (`GET /nav/skeleton` → `/#/skeletons/:id`) — the on-chain element whose PATH_REF path IS the visit history. The **amber traffic-light dot is gone** (2026-07-24 7th pass — the whole bar is the park control now (`.dock-bar--park`), which was only ever a touch affordance since hover parks the widget on the way out; it shares the panel's coat with **no top/bottom border/hairline** so it merges into the list body above it); parked, the **head glyph** (`.dock-side-head` — a **brown-8** icon sitting flat on the plaque, 20px and zero-padded since the 8th pass so it fills its band right under the well; it was a tiny brown-4 mark before) takes that bottom spot, **exactly `--dock-bar-h` (30px) tall like the header it replaces**, so the list box below it keeps its offset across presentations. The list runs **oldest→newest top-to-bottom** (the natural history order — oldest at the top, **newest at the bottom just above the header/head**) with the scroll pinned to the **bottom** (newest) in both presentations. The list (`.stack-list`) is an **inset brown-2 scroll well** (2026-07-24 — the floor spent part of that day at brown-3 and came **back to `--brown-2`**, reading as a soft recess under the plaque rather than a dark trough — a deeper-reading recess since the plaque went paler on 2026-08-17; the rim stayed brown-4): **`--brown-2`** background behind the rows (and behind the scrollbar track), margin all around (plaque reveal — **8px against each frieze band**, 6px on the sides, since a carved wave needs more air than a flat plaque edge; the numbers are identical in both faces or the unravel stops landing in place), a **1px `--brown-4` border** rimming the box — the same ink as the widget's own outer border — and **`--radius-md` rounded corners** that also clip the scrolling rows; its own `is-parked` modifier narrows it to the column (centered chips, hidden scrollbar — the vertical padding and `gap` are shared with the expanded face). The panel **shrinks to fit its step bundle in BOTH presentations** (2026-07-24, 7th pass — expanded used to take its full cap as a FIXED height with the steps bottom-aligned via `margin-top:auto`, which left a dead field between the frieze band and the items): it is only as tall as its steps need and stops growing at **`--dock-stack-h` (63vh)**, where the list scrolls instead — the stack's **70% share** of the 90vh side band it splits with the pins widget's 30% (`--dock-pins-h`, 27vh; both were an even 45vh until 2026-07-24 — the history is the long fast-growing list of the pair, so it took the lion's share). Verified at 900px tall: cap 567px, well `top 33 → bottom 506`, bands at `top 0` and `top 509`, pixel-identical parked and expanded. Both lists are **flex columns in both presentations** with the shared `gap: var(--side-item-gap)` — never block flow, whose margins would collapse where flex gaps do not (that mismatch is exactly what threw the two faces off each other's rhythm). The mid-gap to the pins widget below is preserved by the 70/30 split adding up to the same 90vh (measured 42px at 900px tall — the two never touch). Plaque = **`--plaque-coat`** (a `--light-cream` sheet under a 30% `--grey-3` veil, **2026-08-17** user ask — shared with the nav bar, the left drawer and the other side widget; flat `--brown-1` before it) (header `.dock-bar` included — one uniform coat, and the header HAS to follow or a line appears where the whole point is that there is none) with a **thin classic 1px Quasar brown-4 outer border** (`--brown-4`, 2026-07-24 — the same ink as the well's rim inside it, so widget and well are drawn with one line over the brown-2 floor) on its **left and bottom** edges in both presentations — the **top is bare** since the widget moved to `top: 0` (its frieze band, wearing the crown strip's own brown-3 lip, is what meets the screen edge there); its right edge sits bare at the very **screen edge** (`right: 0`). Beside the downward drop shadow it also casts the shared **`--shadow-side-edge`** off its **left** edge (2026-07-24) — a slight leftward shadow with no vertical offset, in **both** presentations (the rule is state-agnostic), matching the pins widget below and the nav bar's `.tack-slot` so the whole right-edge column reads as one raised strip lying on the page. Presentation is **hover-driven** (2026-07-24 — the `.dock-side-tab` folder handle is gone): resting the pointer anywhere on the parked column **expands** the panel after a **150ms intent delay** (so a pointer flung at the screen edge doesn't pop it open), and **mouse-leave parks it back**; the head glyph and the header bar itself keep their taps for touch screens, where hover doesn't exist. **Minimize-only — no close** (it has no nav-bar opener, so it docks as a permanent top-edge fixture, defaults to parked, and can only park, never be dismissed). The crown strip runs on unbroken to the LEFT of the widget (the stack covers only its own column), so nothing needs to inset for it. Each entry is the **ONE mutating side-bar item** (`layout/SidePanelItem.vue`, shared with the pins widget, 2026-07-24), flipped by the widget's presentation. Expanded face: `MicroChip` + remembered real title + "visited x ago" + a minimal "by <username>" author line (resolved once per element step via `refs/summary`, expanded only), with the square kind button **palette-inverted** (solid kind-color fill, brown-1 icon) on the right and the checkpoint flag on the badges slot; the step you are **at** wraps block + button together in a **kind-colored bubble** (soft accent fill + accent rim, rounded rectangle). Parked face: a flat rounded **Quasar button** (32px wide × `--side-item-h` tall — a vertical key, not a square, since the parked column only clears ~35px of well (28px until the 8th pass tightened the well's side margins)), tinted with the step's own system color (page steps fall back to ink) on its outline + icon over a **`--grey-3`** face (brown-1 until 2026-08-18's grey walk); the current step (`.is-current`) inverts to a solid chip filled with that color + a `--grey-3` icon. **Both faces are exactly `--side-item-h` tall**, so a step unravels at the very level its parked chip sat on (see `SidePanelItem`). The stack layers its own tints on both faces (`is-past` fades, `is-future` italic-soft, checkpoint titles coral — expanded only, the chip has no title). Click-to-jump routes through `navStore.jumpTo` (return halo fires); an expanded row also parks the widget so the destination lands in full view, while a parked chip jumps without changing the presentation. |

Its skin was the bar's copy unchanged until **2026-08-22**, when both took **`NodeMini`'s ROUND PILL** (user ask — see the NavigationBar row for the material): 24px `--grey-3` face, `--grey-5` ring + seam, `--grey-8` 12px `push_pin`, a 2px **`--red-13`** base curving along the bottom arc, the base closing into a full red ring with the pushpin as ink and a 22° tip while the current element is pinned). `overflow: hidden` clips the hairline in the 42px parked state, exactly as the drawer's does. **The move cost no request**: `pinnable` reads the same route parse (`currentTarget`) the bar did, and `isCurrentPinned` is an O(pins) match against rows this widget already holds — where the bar had to ask `GET /pins/check` on every route change; `onTack` pins/unpins, reloads, and emits `changed` so MainLayout can bump the bar's fallback copy. Verified at 1440×900: widget `top 625 → bottom 900`, footer row `868 → 900` = the bar's own row, chip 24px centred at x 1407.5…1431.5 inside the 42px column. Capped at `--dock-pins-h` (**27vh** — the pinned set's **30% share** of the 90vh side band, against the stack's 70%; both were an even 45vh until 2026-07-24, and a short curated list needs far less room than the history), shrink-to-fit, its list scrolling inside the cap. Verified at 900px tall after the 2026-08-02 move: `top 625 → bottom 900` (the window floor), the `.pins-footer` row `868 → 900` = exactly the 32px bar's box, tack chip 24px centred at x 1407.5…1431.5 inside the 42px column, pixel-identical parked and expanded. Same flat brown-1 plaque (header `.dock-bar` included) with a **thin classic 1px brown-4 outer border** (`--brown-4`, 2026-07-24 — the same ink as the well's rim inside it) on the top and left edges only in both presentations; the bottom AND right sit bare — the widget is at the very **screen edge** (`right: 0`). Beside the upward drop shadow it also casts the shared **`--shadow-side-edge`** off its **left** edge (2026-07-24) — a slight leftward shadow with no vertical offset, in **both** presentations (the rule is state-agnostic), matching the stack widget above and the nav bar's `.tack-slot` right below, so the column reads as one raised strip from the screen's top corner down through the footer. Presentation is **hover-driven** like the stack's (2026-07-24 — the `.dock-side-tab` folder handle is gone): hovering the parked column expands the panel after a 150ms intent delay, mouse-leave parks it back; the head glyph and the header bar itself keep their taps for touch screens. **Minimize-only** (no close — with no nav-bar toggle left, a full close would strand it) and it **defaults to** parked. The widget covers no bar buttons in either presentation (it lies over the bar's right end, and `.nav-right--railed` reserves that width so every control stays clear). Expanded face: micro chip + summary title + "pinned x ago" (the API's `pinned_at`, now the pin moment's `time_utc`) + "by <username>" (the summary's `author`), the square kind button **palette-inverted** on the right, and hover-revealed copy-`[[pathos:…]]` + unpin micro-buttons on the actions slot; the pin you are **currently viewing** wraps in the **kind-colored bubble**. A cheap reactive route match (`isCurrent`, no extra request) against the loaded pin rows drives the state. Reloads on the `refresh-key` prop so tack pins/unpins show up live. |
| `layout/SidePanelItem.vue` | ⭐ The **ONE mutating stack/pins side-bar item** (2026-07-24) — fuses the old expanded-row and collapsed-rail-chip renders into a single per-kind component. `collapsed` prop picks the face (driven by the host widget's `is-parked` presentation since the 2026-07-24 third pass): **parked chip** (flat rounded `q-btn`, **32px wide × `--side-item-h` tall** — a vertical key rather than a square, because the parked column (`--dock-rail-w`, 42px) only clears ~35px of well while the height has to match the row's; kind color from `kinds.js` on outline + icon, `current` inverts to solid fill + `--grey-3` icon (brown-1 until 2026-08-18's grey walk), tooltip) or **informative row** (`MicroChip` — plain type icon for kind-less page steps — · title if available · time-ago · "by <username>", with the same button palette-inverted on the right at 26px; `current` wraps the whole row in a **kind-colored bubble**: rounded rectangle, soft `rgba(accent, .14)` fill + accent rim, derived from the kind's hex accent in-component). **Both faces are locked to `--side-item-h` (36px)** (2026-07-24, 7th pass — they were 28px vs ~45px, so a list re-flowed as it unravelled): the row is `height: --side-item-h` with `box-sizing: border-box` and its **text compacted to fit** (head line `line-height: 1` around the 18px chip, 1px info gap, `.side-item__meta` at `0.64em`/`line-height: 1` ≈ 9px — 28px of content inside 3px padding + 1px border) while the chip was **grown** to meet it. Items carry **no vertical margins** — the host list's `gap: var(--side-item-gap)` (4px) spaces them in both faces, since flex gaps don't collapse the way block-flow margins do. Net effect: item *N* occupies the identical band parked or expanded (verified Δtop = 0 across every row of both widgets). `badges` slot (stack's checkpoint flag) + `actions` slot (pins' copy/unpin) let each panel keep its extras; emits `activate` — the row/chip's single click surface (the chip is deliberately non-routing here). |

Nothing changed here for that; `top: 0` is what both asks wanted and only the rail's z moved. The rail stays click-through paint, so the covered pixels keep taking taps. **It also casts NOTHING** since the same ask ("no shadow pls"): the `6px 0 22px` cast that washed the rail's underside, the nav bar's plaque and the page is gone, and its `border-right` states the edge alone. **Since 2026-08-02 it does the same at the bottom** (user ask): **`bottom: 0` at z 3120**, the full window height, lying OVER the nav bar (3110) instead of stopping at its top edge — safe because the bar's left cluster is empty whenever the drawer stands (the burger is `v-if`'d out of `.nav-left` and drawn by the drawer's own `.drawer-footer` instead). The content is a flex column: `.drawer-scroll` taking everything above the pinned **`.drawer-footer`** (`min-height: 0`) — and its list's FIRST `.drawer-frieze` is flush with the window's top edge since 2026-08-17 (user ask: "no gap between the screen top border and itself"), which took zeroing BOTH Quasar's `.q-list--padding` top and the band's own `margin-top` (the list keeps its bottom padding). The footer is the nav bar's row rebuilt inside the drawer: `--plaque-coat`, a **`--grey-6`** `border-top` on the bar's own pixel — the SAME ink as the bar's own since that day's palette ask closed the seam, and the `bar-lip` witness in `fsck --static` now holds all three copies of that edge — then the 41px `.drawer-burger-slot` + 1px hairline. That slot is painted **transparent** (user ask, same session) so the drawer's own coat shows through where a `--brown-4` rail block used to be, and the chip in it is a **rounded square filling the slot** (100% × 100% inside the slot's 4px pad, `--radius-sm`, `--grey-9` face + `--grey-3` glyph) rather than the 24px circle it was; it only CLOSES the drawer. ⚠ NavigationBar's twin chip (mobile only) keeps the round brown figure — the two are never on screen together. The profile block and its mask chip walked with them (`--grey-9` name, `--grey-3` ring). **Styled from an unscoped block** — the `pathos-drawer` class lands on Quasar's `.q-drawer__content`, which doesn't carry the SFC scope attribute (see specs/gotchas.md). | `shared/ElementFlyoutHost.vue` | **The flyout viewer mount** (2026-08-04 as `media/MediaViewerHost.vue`; renamed with the 2026-08-17 fusion) — MainLayout mounts it once; it renders every live `ElementFlyout` window (`v-show`, not `v-if`: a parked video, a scrolled article or a drafted comment in an embedded postcard survives minimize/restore) + `MediaTabsBar` unconditionally. `display: contents` — every child is `position: fixed`. |
| `media/MediaViewerWindow.vue` | **RETIRED 2026-08-17 — fused into `shared/ElementFlyout.vue`**, which took this window's SHELL AND BEHAVIORS WHOLE (coat, rim, corners, teal illumination, drag, rims, fit, foot, park) and added the element/skeleton view switch + post/ref targets; see that row. What follows is the shell's record, still true of the fused window: the rounded box born in the arena and free to roam the screen: a SINGLE `--grey-4` coat inside a soft `--grey-6` rim, header included, house `.dock-bar` with the docks' traffic lights (red close / yellow minimize / green fullscreen) left, centred **kind glyph + file name** as one group (`--grey-9` ink over `--grey-8` glyph, set in `--font-display`/Nasalization at 0.72em — the wide face buys back with a smaller size what it spends on width, and the ellipsis lives on the label so the glyph can never be what gets truncated; both the name and the glyph come from `utils/mediaKind.js` — `titleOf` and `iconFor`, the same two the parked tab reads, so a window is its tab at full size and web media reads `PROVIDER :: name`) — the header carries nothing else now (the action cluster moved to the foot, 2026-08-05 user ask) — the side padding that used to mirror that cluster went ASYMMETRIC to match (2026-08-05, second user ask that day: the title was clipping), `70px` on the left (just clears the traffic-light cluster) and `12px` on the right, so the title gets the rest of the bar instead of being boxed into a padding sized for an icon cluster that isn't there anymore; under the header the flyout's band on a `--frieze-bar-base: var(--grey-8)` plaque with `--frieze-bar-wave-two: var(--brown-1)` (both stated rather than defaulted); then the well (4px pad, `container-type: size`) holding `MediaViewerBody`, whose `.mv-body` draws **nothing of its own** — no rim, no floor, the `--grey-4` coat straight through, so the MEDIA is the only thing in the window that is not the coat and the only edges left are the window's rim and the frieze band. It got there by elimination over five user asks the same day: `--grey-8` rim → `--grey-6` rim → `--grey-4` floor + `--grey-5` rim → a solid topless `--grey-5` plate → uniform. Each pass was smoother than the last and the end of that line is no step at all ("uniform look"); the caption ink went `--grey-9` → `--grey-10` while the floor was dark and back to `--grey-9`; and last the **foot**, a 22px ledge (grown from 18px, 2026-08-05) carrying the **action cluster** (`--grey-8`, `--grey-10` on hover) at its left — `adjust` (the drawer's own NODES glyph) → `/nodes/:id`, a 1px `--grey-6` hairline, then copy-link share, `content_copy` → the **pathchain path** (`nodes/<hash>`), and `push_pin` → a `pinService` toggle wearing `--accent` while held and emitting `pins-changed` up through the host to MainLayout's `pinsRefreshKey` (moved down off the header and share swapped with the node-page button, both 2026-08-05 user asks) — and the node's tally at its right end — flat glyph + count in `--font-display`, and unlike NodeMini's read-only pair these VOTE (`nodeInteractionService`, the node page's own toggle rule, `--positive`/`--negative` ink on your own vote, `navStore.recordAction` so it lands in the pathchain like any other). `FOOT_H` in `utils/mediaFit.js` keeps the fit engine in step with the ledge: a window sized by the old constants clips its media by exactly the height of what was added (a `BODY_RIM` lived there too while the content box had a rim). **The tones moved THREE TIMES on 2026-08-05** (three user asks). First the header's darker `--grey-10` cap was dropped and its tone pushed down into the band, turning two stacked plates into one `--grey-9` field with a single dark line across it. Then the coat crossed to the LIGHT half of the scale — `--grey-4` (#e0e0e0) — and every light-on-dark decision in the window inverted with it: the name, the share glyph and the body's embed caption are dark neutrals now. Last, the RIM followed: `--grey-8` was a half-step line while the coat was dark and a 3.2:1 black outline on the light one — the darkest mark on the window, drawn round the outside, competing with the band it is supposed to sit under — so the border went to a newly declared **`--grey-6`** (#9e9e9e, 1.9:1 on the coat), an edge one shade off the face rather than an outline painted on it. That is what the user's "bold smooth metallic" ask comes to: brushed metal has no outline, and the drop shadow is what separates the box from the page, so the rim only has to say where the metal stops. The band kept its job through all four states: one dark strip, warm wave, doing alone what a coat change used to do. Geometry = inline style off `viewer.rect`; spawn placement = `probeNaturalSize` → `fitRect` (maximize the binding dimension in the arena, cascade by index); an **arena watcher re-clamps stale rects** on viewport/rotate. `is-max` = true fullscreen (100vw/100vh, **z 3200** over the drawer's 3120; normal z = `windows.zOf('media:<id>')`, interleaving with the docks) with a ~240ms geometry ease that never animates spawns or gestures. Trigger: **NodeMini's header corner button** (the old decorative `open_in_new` glyph, now `open_in_full`) → `mediaViewers.spawn(node)` on every NodeMini surface — feed-card block embeds, the flyout, LabelUsages, FileTreeNode, path/link viewers. |
| `media/MediaViewerBody.vue` | The viewer's **per-kind face** (one `face` computed): embed → `EmbedFrame` with the caption-corrected height budget (`--media-max-h: calc(100cqh − 22px)` — the caption line would otherwise overflow the well by exactly its own height) and the caption recolored light for the dark floor (player mode centres box + caption; page mode fills); image/video contained + centred (a ≤300px-long-side image renders at natural size instead of stretching); audio = 90% centred bar; **markdown = a light `--grey-1` reading pane** rendering `MarkdownBody :plain-refs :breaks="false"` under the global `.md-rendered` skin, with a tiny mono corner chip flipping **raw ⇄ pretty** (raw = verbatim `<pre>`); non-md text and bare NOTEs = the raw pane; binary/url-less/over-1MB-text = a centred download card (kind glyph, name, `.ext · size`, download link). |
| `media/MediaTabsBar.vue` | ⭐ **IT CARRIES THE PLATFORM'S FRIEZE SINCE 2026-08-22** (user ask: "take the cyan-indigo frieze bar from the post cards and incrust it on the top navigation header of the whole screen") — `.media-tabs__frieze` is the POST CARD'S band moved here whole: a `FriezeBar slim`, `--grey-8` plaque, **cyan→indigo** `--frieze-bar-wave-two-paint` ramp (⚠ **inverted 2026-08-24** — user ask: "instead of goin indigo to cyan, make it cyan to indigo"; indigo-led until then, which is the direction the POST CARD's pair settled on 2026-08-10 and still runs, so the two surfaces now ramp OPPOSITE ways and what they share is the recipe, not the direction. Note the two halves moved in OPPOSITE directions from where the ask started, the line toward the light end and the writing past the ink; that spread is what keeps a 1px rim and a 12px word both legible on one light face. Glyph per media kind via `utils/mediaKind.js#iconFor` (page embeds `public` vs players `smart_display` — what restoring the tab gives you, not who hosts it), and its LABEL from the same module's `titleOf`, the one naming seam the window header also reads: web media is **`PROVIDER :: name`** in caps (2026-08-05, user ask) — "WIKIPEDIA :: Talavera pottery", "YOUTUBE :: 6gm9IWUuus0" — the caps sorting a strip of tabs by source at a glance and the name telling two tabs from the same site apart. The name is dug out of the URL (`webNameOf`: last non-routing path segment, `_`→space, `?v=` when the whole path routes), because the embed descriptor's `title` is the RULE's generic label ("YouTube video player") and would make every YouTube tab read the same; a node's own `title` outranks both. **The row fills right→left** and stops at **50vw**: `store.parked` reversed (newest leftmost, so every older tab keeps its slot and the newest is the end that stays visible), pinned by `margin-left: auto` on the first tab — NOT `justify-content: flex-end`, which strands overflowing content past the scroll container's start ([gotchas.md](../../specs/gotchas.md)) — tabs `flex: 0 1 auto` shrinking to a 46px icon floor, and only then a hidden-scrollbar horizontal scroller. ⚠ **THE TAB IS CONTENT-TALL AND THE ROW STANDS CLEAR OF THE STACK COLUMN, 2026-08-24** (two user asks: "a lot denser, removing top inner padding and making bottom padding very thin", then "make the first tab on the right appear a little away from the stack sidebar"). The flat `height: 22px` + `align-items: center` — ≈5px of dead face over the glyph and 5px under — is `padding: 0 10px 2px` + `line-height: 1` now, so the **12px kind glyph sets the line** and the only vertical space declared anywhere is the 2px under the writing: **15px measured** (12 + 2 + rim), the rail's own height, a tab hanging exactly as far below the band as the band is tall. No top pad is the READING, not the saving — the tab has no top border either, so the writing starts on the band's underside, metal into metal, which is the argument the flares make at the corners. Hover's +3px reach is spelled in the pad (`padding-bottom: 2 → 5px`) since there is no height left to grow; the row's 5px bottom pad still covers that plus the press dip. (5) The PAD-REMOVAL ASK ("removing the inner padding between the svg and the top and bottom borders of the friezebar") zeroed the pad through **`--frieze-bar-pad`, the dial FriezeBar published for it** (the horizontal reading of `--frieze-bar-v-pad`), and the band went **13px** — 1 rule + 11 ink + 1 rule, the mask overhanging the 11px inner so the ink stands directly on the rules (⚠ 15px with pad 0 redraws the gap in plate paint — 13 with overhang or 15 with pads, no third state). So `--media-tabs-band` is `calc(18px − rim)`: total **18px** = lead 2 + band 13 + pad 2 + rim 1. (6) And the LIPS CAST ONTO THE BAND (".media-tabs__frieze::after", an inset box-shadow pair OVER plate and motif alike — an inset shadow on the element itself would paint under the wave layers and stencil at every stroke); the tone walked `--grey-6` → `--grey-8` → `--grey-4` → `--grey-9` → `--grey-7` → `--grey-5` and SETTLED BACK ON **`--grey-6`** ("ok no, grey-6" — seven asks, the whole scale seen, landing on the rules' own ink at the walk's 0.75 alpha), mapping the dial: lighter than the plate = the lips' pale bloom, at/under it = a true shadow that reads only where it crosses the motif. ⚠ The 08-21 trio match ("same height as the drawer's top frieze bar") is SUPERSEDED — the rail overhangs the side bands by 3px of click-through paint; what survives is the shape (total pinned, band = remainder). Every top-anchored surface follows through `--media-tabs-h` untouched. ⭐ **2026-08-30 SUPERSEDED THE EXCHANGE'S HEADER HALF** (user ask: "grey veil on top of the inner frieze bar … removing it" + "change the double svg pattern to the single-SVG one … copy the friezes on the main feed container"): the LIPS' CAST is DELETED — `.media-tabs__frieze::after` and its anchoring `position: relative` are gone (on the `--grey-8` plate the lighter grey-6 wash bloomed across the whole band: the "veil") — and **`slim` is back on**: ONE wave, mask `b`, the same pattern the feed container's bands run, so both surfaces draw one motif and nothing traded back. Wave ONE's dials left with the layer (slim v-ifs it out). The pixel-drawn geometry survives the variant change — band 13px at `auto 13px`, pad 0, total 18px — because `slim` reads the `--frieze-bar-fit`/`--frieze-bar-carve` dials since this ask (its 117%/halved-carve become the defaults, so dial-less slim hosts are untouched). Coloring never moved in that pass: grey-8 plaque, the one cyan→indigo ramp. ⭐ **AND THE SAME DAY'S LATER ASKS FINALLY MOVED IT — AND UNFRAMED IT** ("making its background color the same as the bar, and re-painting the gradient part with grey-4", then "pls try grey-6 and remove the top and bottom borders of the inner frieze bar"): plaque = **the rail's own `--plaque-coat`** — legal in the dial because FriezeBar spends `--frieze-bar-base` in a `background:` shorthand, the one slot a layer list may fill — so plate and rail composite to the same rgb and the plate VANISHES; wave = **flat `--grey-6`** (`--grey-4` held one pass; grey-6 is the rail's line ink, so meander and rim are one tone), the cyan→indigo ramp DELETED with its `-paint` dial (the flat dial is the drawn ink now, not the never-seen fallback it had been since 08-22); and the band's two 1px grey-6 rules DELETED — **a bare meander floating on the chrome**, the rim the bar's one remaining horizontal line. ⚠ Geometry held, meaning turned: band 13px but the LAYER is the full 13px (no borders eating it) and the `auto 13px` mask fits EXACTLY, its two empty edge rows painting in coat — the old "no third state" law is void with plate = coat and no rules. ⚠ VALUE-INVERTED pair (ink darker than plate): the carve must stay off, per the law the feed's inverted bars named — moot today (pixel-drawn law has it off) but the constraint to check before ever re-lighting it. ⚠ The FOOTER TRAIL (`.nav-frieze`) took the header's OLD coloring earlier the same day and was NOT in these asks — the window's two horizontal bands diverge until an ask says otherwise. ⭐ **AND THE SITTING'S LAST ASK SWAPPED THE TWO BANDS** ("invert the frieze pattern on the top nav bar with the bottom footer nav bar one") — the footer trail had preserved this band's pre-recolor dress, so the swap RESTORES it here: **grey-8 plate, teal-11 flat under the cyan→indigo `-paint` ramp**, unframed (the rules stayed deleted — a bare dark stripe on the cream rail, the trail's old presentation), while the recolor's grey-6-on-coat moved downstairs to `.nav-frieze`. Witness: `flow-feed-cream.mjs` (headBandH 13 / layer 13 / `auto 13px` exact / railBottom 18 / ONE wave / `::after` boxShadow `none` / headWaveInk teal-11 / headPlate grey-8 / headWaves gradient-true / headRules 0px 0px). |
| `posts/PostCard.vue` | Card surface for a post |
| `posts/FeedHeadBox.vue` | ⭐ **The public feed's head, as a box you can move** (2026-08-06, user ask) — what `.feed-stream__head` became. **THREE changes and one addition.** (1) **It is grabbed by its own inner header.** The box opens with a `.feed-head__bar` — grip glyph pinned left, `Public Feed` centred in `nasalization` and `--indigo-10` (the tone the media tabs write in, since this chrome borrows their corners rather than the cards' ink) — and that bar is the ONLY drag surface, the division of labour every floating window here already makes (`.dock-bar`, `MediaViewerWindow`). `touch-action: none` on it is load-bearing: without it a phone claims the vertical gesture for a scroll and the pointer stream dies mid-drag. The bar is also `tabindex="0"` with **↑/↓ (12px), Home and End** — a box that can only be placed by dragging cannot be placed at all without a pointer. (2) **It slides up and down between the frieze bars.** `position: absolute` against a now-`relative` `.feed-stream-pane`, which IS the field between the two bars, so `left/right: 0` spans it lip to lip and the pane's own height is the travel — no measuring, no arena. Vertical only (there is no horizontal freedom to give), clamped to `EDGE = FLARE + 1 = 12px` off each end, which is exactly the daylight the corner sweeps need before `.feed-container__body`'s `overflow: hidden` would clip them — it must CLEAR the sweep's reach, so it moved with the fillets when they grew. Bounds are re-measured per gesture and by a `ResizeObserver` on the box AND its parent, so a restored offset that is illegal at the current window size is pulled back rather than left off-screen. Gesture hygiene is `useMediaWindowGestures`': pointer capture on the bar, body `userSelect`/`cursor` parked and restored, one idempotent teardown that release, cancel and unmount all reach. (3) **Its four corners flare into the bars** — `MediaTabsBar`'s concave fillets, four of them: the face sweeping into the bar through a quarter-disc carved out of the corner DIAGONALLY OPPOSITE the joint, with two extra gradient stops drawing the rim along the arc in the rim's own tone, so one continuous `--indigo-4` runs bar → flare → edge → flare → bar. **The geometry is DERIVED, not copied** (2026-08-06, second ask — thickening the rim 1px → 2px broke every corner until it was re-solved). With the border's outer edge at `y=0`, the box's side edge at `x=0`, `R` the sweep radius and `T = --fhead-rim-w`: the ring runs `R−T/2 → R+T/2`; its circle sits at `(R, T/2 − R)`, both coordinates forced — the bottom-most point must land on the border's **centre line** (`y = T/2`), not its outer edge, or the arc *touches* the border instead of continuing it, and the left-most must land on `x = 0` so the line dies at the bar's face, half the stroke clipped, which is what a line disappearing behind an edge looks like; so the element is `width: R`, `height: R + T`, `top: -(R + T)`, gradient centred `(R, T/2)`. **The offset names the border it reaches over** — an absolutely positioned child is placed against the PADDING box, which is why 1px → 2px moved it by two ([gotchas](../../specs/gotchas.md)). Verify by measurement, not by eye: at `x = R` the ring must cover exactly `y ∈ [0, T]`. `R` went **9 → 11** with the thickening (a heavier line wants a longer arc, or the sweep reads as a blunt notch — and `EDGE` had to follow), and **the stop ramps 0.2px → 0.6px, which is what stopped the curve stair-stepping**: hard stops do not antialias, so a 1px arc broke into dashes exactly where it ran near-parallel to the pixel grid. The weight survives because what reads as the edge is the MID-ramp (9.9 → 12.1 = 2px). `--fhead-face`/`--fhead-rim`/`--fhead-rim-w` exist so the gradients follow the face and the derivation follows the thickness (a gradient cannot inherit `background`). **Material:** `--grey-4` — **THE PLATE'S OWN** (user ask) — with `2px --indigo-4` top and bottom, NO side edges (the fillets carry the line round), a **discrete cast** (`0 7px 14px -7px` at .45 plus `0 -5px 12px -8px` at .28: mostly down, a hint up because the stream passes on both sides of a box that parks in the middle of it, and **both spreads are negative by exactly half the blur so lateral reach is ZERO** — the box's side edges ARE the frieze bars and any sideways travel would smear onto their motif), lifting ONE step to `--grey-3` with a deeper cast while held. It spent the first ask of that day on the post card's `--grey-3`, on the argument that a thing you can pick up cannot be the plate it moves over. Depth answers that better than tone: a heavier edge and a shadow say *above the plate* where a lighter coat only said *a different plate* — and the gain is at the CORNERS, since face and plate being one tone makes the carved and filled quarters indistinguishable, leaving only the swept line to state the shape. The box flows out of the bars instead of sitting between them, which a `--grey-3` patch could never do. (Knock-on: the chat log's floor was `--grey-4` and would have drawn nothing, so log and composer are both `--grey-1` now — one pale surface in two parts, told apart by their rims.) **(4) THE BOX IS THREE THIN BANDS** — handle 18px, a ONE-LINE body 30px, a label lane 19px, **71px all in, down from 119** (2026-08-06, three compression asks in a row). The body was two rows a side; both are one row now — the seat stopped stacking over the input, and the caption (`.feed-stream__sub`, "Every POST instance, newest first.") went entirely, the last of the head's prose and a claim the box would have had to keep in step with a SORT control on the same line. **(5) TWO INNER VERTICAL FRIEZE BARS** (user ask) stand at `FLARE` from each side — precisely where each corner sweep's tangent turns horizontal and the flat border begins, so the sweep comes off the container's big bar and LANDS on a post and the box reads as a framed span rather than a slab wedged between two bars. The pair is also the platform's TWO FRIEZE FAMILIES STACKED — the feed colorway's deep end under the crown strip's own plaque tone — rather than a palette invented for one 9px bar, and the spread is enormous by frieze standards (Material 800 under a 50) because a slim inner bar has no lip and no rolled edge, so the motif is the only thing separating plate from wave. So the box reads in THREE CONSECUTIVE STEPS with one job each: **-7 the frame, -8 the two inner posts, -9 everything written between them** — lightest at the outside, deepest at the marks, so the eye lands on what the box says and the structure holding it stays structure. `$indigo-7` and `$indigo-9` were both minted for this. **The edge is also ASYMMETRIC** (user ask): `2px` top, `3px` bottom. Not by thinning the top — the 2px is the thickening asked for two passes earlier — but by weighting the BASE, where the cast falls and where the stream runs up against the box; the post cards wore exactly this shape (a hairline box on a 2px foot) until their bed went lighter than the card. It re-solves the BOTTOM pair of fillets on its own — height `R+T` = 14, offset `-14`, centre `12.5`, ring `9.5 → 12.5` — and leaves the top pair alone, which is the derivation paying for itself. The active-filter chip moved here out of the lens row; the funnel stays there and takes the lens buttons' `is-on` plaque instead, so the control still says the stream is filtered without also having to say what by. It scrolls rather than ellipsing, because a filter whose name you cannot read is not one you can trust. **(7) THE SEAT AND THE FIELD:** a **seat** (`EntityAvatar` at 24px + `OrgLogoChip`, NO NAME — three asks trimmed it, the role sub-line first and the name last, leaving the face to carry the seat; the badge stays because it is the only visible thing left saying WHICH org this seat belongs to, which was the point of putting a seat here, and both elements wear the full name/org tooltip) that CURRENTLY references claude in the Dream Operator's Garage, then an **inert filter/chat field** on the same line: a `Filter by…` input and one button, both `disabled` — no store, no service, no events; the surface placed before the wiring, and `disabled` is the honest way to say so. The MESSAGE LOG that sat above them (`No messages yet.`) is GONE (user ask): it was a summary of a conversation that does not exist, the one part of this box making a claim rather than holding a place. The button wears an oversized `filter_alt` rather than a send arrow (user ask) — 17px in a 20px box, so the mark reaches the rim on both axes and the control reads as one solid glyph; it is the same funnel the label lens wears, which is the point, the two are one gesture at two scales. The line's spring is the INPUT (`1 1 auto`, min-width 0): face, badge and button are fixed, the name yields first (it ellipses), and whatever the half has left is the field. The seat is looked up, not hardcoded: `refService.search('entities', 'claude')` once per page load (module-cached PROMISE, so two mounts share one request), taking the MASK for the org badge and the ROOT person for the face and the name — the same recomposition `entityProfileService.getIdentityCards` does for the feed, since a mask's own display name is the `"<person> @ <org>"` string that seam exists to take apart. Entity ids differ per install (claude's garage mask is 23 locally), which is why the reference is a name + an org regex at the top of the script and a static stub behind it. **The SECOND HALF** is the stream's own lenses through the `controls` slot — the box owns none of them. FeedStream owns every round trip; the box is a face and a field. **The LANE split 70/30 later the same day** (user ask): a BROOM button at the far left (`cleaning_services`, `sweep` emit), the active chip scroller (~70%, `#labels` slot), and a ruled-off TRASH section (30%, `#trash` slot, faint bin glyph as its name) — the box owns the geometry and the disabled aesthetic's home; the stream owns the semantics: closed label chips land there instead of deleting, stand as VETOES against later verdicts (stripped before every applySpec branch, the say line appending "· trash kept out"), restore by click, and the broom clears both sections in one press with the filter change actually applied. A spoken reset leaves the trash standing; only the broom empties it.  **THE BOTTOM EDGE HAS ITS OWN TONE** (2026-08-07, user ask): `--fhead-rim-b`, split off from `--fhead-rim` the same way `--fhead-rim-w` / `--fhead-rim-w-b` (2px / 3px) already split the WEIGHT. Same reasoning read in colour: the bottom is the heavier edge because the box stands ON the stream, and a heavier edge in the SAME ink reads as more of the same line, where a lighter ink at more weight reads as a base the box rests on. It **walked `--indigo-5` → `-6` → `--indigo-9` across three asks that day** and the walk is the argument: -5 made the base the box's brightest edge, -6 (the pure hue) was the first setting where it read as the DEEPER of the two, and -9 is the tone the box's whole INTERIOR is built from — so the frame is a `--indigo-7` LID and a `--indigo-9` FOOT, and the foot is made of the same ink as everything the box is built out of. ⚠ **The bottom edge is drawn in THREE places** — this border plus BOTH bottom fillet gradients (`--bl`, `--br`), whose colour stops carry the line around the corner — and all three read the one dial, which is the only reason the sweep doesn't change colour halfway (verified by measurement: bottom border + both bottom flares all `rgb(40,53,147)`, top border + both top flares `rgb(57,73,171)`); the TOP pair keeps `--fhead-rim`. ⚠ The feed container's frieze bars took `--indigo-5` on their inner-facing lips in the ask right after this edge FIRST moved, deliberately as one mark — **that pairing is broken by three levels now** and the lips were left where they were; don't restore either to match the other without an ask.  ⚠ **`remeasure` REFUSES TO MEASURE A HIDDEN BOX** (2026-08-18): the container folds shut now (`FeedPage`'s rail keys) by `display: none`ing `.feed-container__body`, so the box's own ResizeObserver fires with every reading at 0 — and measured through, a zero-height container makes `bounds()` return `{min: EDGE, max: EDGE}`, `clamp` snap `y` to HOME, and the board silently lose the spot it was dragged to while nobody can see it happen. One guard on `offsetHeight`/`parentElement.clientHeight` and the offset survives the fold. ⚠ The guard is for `display: none` specifically — a STRETCH out of the fold gives the body `visibility: hidden` instead, precisely so the box keeps a real height to clamp against while the frame is being pulled open. **`update:center`** (2026-08-18, same pass) is this box's one PER-FRAME report: `y + height/2`, watched off a cached height (`offsetHeight` in a pointermove watcher is a forced layout every frame), carried up through `FeedStream` so the container's fold keys stand on the board's middle line and travel with it. It is deliberately the opposite decision from `update:offset` beside it — that one is release-only because it is PERSISTED; this one is only drawn with, and a mark that caught up on release would lag the board across the whole drag. |

| `posts/FeedHeadBox.vue` + `shared/TalaveraToggle.vue` — **the 2026-08-21 manual-toggle pass** | ⭐ **The board has TWO ARRANGEMENTS now** (five user asks in one sitting), and this row supersedes the older rows' lens-half/two-row-controls/always-present-lane claims. **FULL TALAVERO (default):** header + one band — the talk room is a **GASKET** since **2026-08-22**, when four asks rebuilt it and the carve moved INWARD: a **`--brown-1` panel, 1px `--indigo-5` rim, NO carve, `padding: 3px` on all four sides** ("remueve el efecto ahuevado de la seccion del chat … pero conserva el de la burbuja … ponle bordes indigo-5"; "comprímela … padding muy reducido y uniforme"). It holds **two objects of EQUAL height — 36px each**, and that equality is a hard constraint, not a coincidence: uniform padding only looks uniform if both objects fill it. (1) the **seat column** = a 28px avatar + an 8px box for the `@tala` arc, which sits below the face and **touches its rim** — the arc is **CONCENTRIC with the face** (centre (14,14), baseline r 19.15 = 14 + the measured 5.15px ascender at 6.2px, path 150°→30°, `viewBox` `0 0 28 40` 1:1 with CSS px). Three passes of nudging an offset could not glue it, because the topmost ink is at the string's ENDS while the eye reads the MIDDLE; concentric makes touching structural. (2) the **bubble**, `--fhead-chat-h` **36px**, dialled to that column rather than to its own text: **`--brown-1` face** (it left the indigos), **`--indigo-5` rim**, and the board's ONE concave surface — the room's old carve one size down (`--fhead-chat-carve`), restated on all THREE faces because an inset shadow paints under a child's background. Bubble and room are the SAME tone (1:1), so rim + carve are a pair. **Two lines fit by default**: `3 (rim) + 3.5 (top) + 2 (foot) + 2 × 8.96 × 1.35 = 32.7 ≤ 36`, published as `--fhead-chat-pad-t/-b` + `--fhead-chat-lead`; top and foot are unequal on purpose (ask + the carve falls from the top edge). Room = 3 + 36 + 3 + 2 rims = **44px**. The board's **top edge is `--indigo-8`** since the same day (from -7; its two top fillets follow for free, reading their stops from `--fhead-rim`), the bottom staying `--indigo-9`. *(Superseded 08-21 record: a `--brown-2` CARVED PIT on the label maker's `.label-square__pit` recipe at 0.30 / white lip 0.4 with no rim, holding a ONE-LINE 22px bubble rimmed `--indigo-7`. `rows="1"` and the no-stretch rule still stand.)* Nothing hand-operated renders and the board rests at ~76px. With a lens live the bubble wears **Talavero's STANDING LINE** (`.feed-head__chat-standing`): his last `say` with the lens chips **embedded inline** (the `#labels` slot rendered inside the bubble — labels as his text), growing to `--fhead-chat-max` 64px then scrolling inside; click the line → the field, focused; empty blur / Esc / a fresh verdict / lens death restore the sentence; `say` verdicts skip the 6s transient here and land standing (songs/fails stay transient). **MANUAL:** the toggle pulled DOWN opens `.feed-head__manual` — ONE 28px row under the bubble (`--indigo-9`, `--indigo-7` hairline above) holding the stream's controls (label bar first at `flex: 1 1 auto`, input basis 88px; bundle natural width — the phone arrangement promoted to base, the old mobile overrides deleted) then the lane's four pieces unchanged in name and order: active tray · broom · trash tray (basis `0 1 130px` now) · bin; `.feed-head__lane` itself is deleted and the constant-height doctrine deliberately traded for the `update:height` observer. **THE TOGGLE** (`shared/TalaveraToggle.vue`, new, reusable v-model switch): a vertical 3D talavera handle at the bubble's right on the body's `--indigo-9` — ⭐ **REBUILT 2026-08-22** (four asks, with a photo of a cobalt talavera tile as the reference). **BAR**: no frieze at all — `FriezeBarVertical`, `.tala-toggle__rail` and the import are DELETED; `.tala-toggle__sleeve` IS the bar, a plain **`--indigo-10`** channel (from -9 — the channel is FRAME, and a frame may be the deepest thing on the surface) with **STADIUM ends** (`border-radius: 999px`) and a **1px `--indigo-5` rim** in place of its two inset shadows ("quitándole la sombra interna y poniéndole bordes indigo-5" — the shadows painted depth into a bar the same tone as the body behind it and read as a smear at each end). The frieze went because at this size a patterned strip under a ceramic knob is two talavera objects competing; all the ceramic moved to the knob. **KNOB**: **round** and, after five stripping asks the same day, **FLAT**. It was built as a glazed talavera boss in three zones (convex ring under a radial gradient, concave well with the same gradient reversed, centre hole, specular glint, white top lip, cast shadow); the asks removed the 3D one statement at a time in order of obviousness — glint, lip ("remueve el efecto glossy"), the ring's gradient ("es más brillante arriba que abajo"), the centre hole ("remueve la bolita más oscura que está en el centro"), the cast shadow ("remueve el efecto de sombra … encima de la barrita"). What is left is **two flat concentric discs** — ring `--indigo-6`, well `--indigo-8` at `inset: 20%` under one dark inset lip — with an `--indigo-6` rim matching the bar's, told apart from the `--indigo-10` bar by TONE STEP alone. ⚠ **Nothing states depth on this control now**: if it stops reading as a handle the dial is the ring's tone (away from -10), not a shadow coming back; hover is a tone step (-6 → -5) for the same reason. **THINNER** ("un poquito más delgado"): rail **15 → 12px**, knob **19 → 16px**, lap 2px — they move together because the diameter IS `rail + 2 laps` (⚠ or the knob stops being round), and the rail's old 15 was a pixel-grid constraint from the frieze it no longer carries. Track `min-height` is `knob + 10px`, not the old `knob × 2 + 6`, which at a round knob would have forced the compressed talk band back open. `top`-transition travel, DOWN = on. The thin nasalization word **`manual`** stands VERTICAL (`writing-mode: vertical-rl`, 7px/400) at the toggle's **RIGHT** since 2026-08-22 (`flex-direction: row-reverse`, so the DOM keeps caption-then-control for the accessible name and only the paint order flips; it stood at the LEFT before) in **`--brown-1`** (an `--indigo-8` first pass was invisible on the -9 backdrop), hidden on the phone. State: `manualMode`/`standingSay`/`lensLive` in FeedStream (session-local), props `manual`/`spoken`/`lensLive` + `update:manual` on the box. Verified by `flow-talavero-board.mjs`. |
| `posts/FeedStream.vue` | ⭐ **The feed container's content column** (end of 2026-07-25) — the post stream that had been parked in `FeedPageLegacy.vue`, refitted for the narrow indigo box on `FeedPage` and mounted in `.feed-container__body`. Two changes came with the move, both forced by the column's width: (1) **ONE column, not two** — the legacy page put the stream (`col-md-7`) beside a tall `FeedPostPanel` (`col-md-5`) across the whole page, and Quasar's `col-md-*` breakpoints read the **WINDOW, not the parent**, so inside a 50%-of-track box that split would still fire and leave two cramped strips; (2) the detail panel UNFOLDED IN PLACE — selecting a card rendered the same `FeedPostPanel` directly beneath it. **That unfold is GONE (2026-07-25, fourth pass)** and with it the whole selection machinery (`selected`, `isSelected`, `select`, `keyOf`, the holder's `selectedKey`, `.is-selected` styling, `.feed-stream__detail` and the four `:deep(.feed-post-panel*)` overrides). It was **the one thing on the surface that broke the square rule**: the panel obeyed no ceiling, so a selected card measured **615 card + 570 panel = 1185px against a 615px width — 1.93×** — and being full-bleed and flush underneath it read as the card simply continuing, which is what "the height is way longer than the width" was seeing. It had also become redundant: the card now carries the whole body, the author, the label paths and the tallies, and the panel's remaining extras (node/moment chips, thread provenance) are one click away in the post viewer. The card is no longer a click target at all. **The PANEL came back on 2026-07-26 as a floating box beside the container** (`skeletons/SkeletonFlyout.vue`) — what died was laying it INSIDE the column, not the box itself, and the ceiling argument above is exactly why it may not go back there. The card carried ONE TRIGGER for that box between 2026-08-09 and 2026-08-10: the foot's **`PostMicro` with `:linked="false"`** — a chip that stopped being an anchor so the same press could open the box instead of navigating (a wrapper cannot catch that click; the chip's root carries `@click.stop`, so the listener goes on the chip — see [gotchas](../../specs/gotchas.md)). (The byline's title plate was the second trigger from 2026-07-26 until 08-09 deleted it, together with `.post-square__go`, the byline's open-post link — the title states itself in the CAP and the way into the VIEWER is the flyout's own links.) **THE FOOT CHIP IS A DOOR AGAIN (2026-08-10, user ask)**: a router-link to the POST VIEWER (`/posts/:id`, the kind's own route — the `:linked="false"` and the `@click` both came off), reading **`sym_o_post :: skeleton :: <hash>`** in Nasalization. Three deliberate departures from a stock chip, all borrowed from the CAP one strip up so the card's two ends speak one dialect: the **glyph** is the cap's `sym_o_post` and not the kind's default `edit_note`; the **type word** is `skeleton`, because a post's address is a handle on its skeleton and that is the reading every control around it opens; the **separator** is `::`. Later the same day the type WORD became the cap's `sym_o_orthopedics` DRAWN — the chip's neighbours in this strip are all glyphs and one spelt-out word was the odd thing out. The three ride new `typeLabel` / `typeIcon` / `sep` props on MicroChip (see its row). The card's doors in the cap's lane — `sym_o_orthopedics` routes to `/skeletons/:id`; `open_in_new` opens the post in the ELEMENT FLYOUT beside the feed (since 2026-08-17 the same `select` the references button emits — the embedded postcard steps forward, the skeleton is the box's header switch). `select` has TWO live callers since 2026-08-17 — the foot's REFERENCES button (2026-08-10, below) and the cap's `open_in_new`, merged onto one emit — and it SPAWNS a flyout viewer window (FeedPage → `flyoutViewers.spawnPost`); the `selectedId` prop became **`openIds`** (the store's open post list) when one box became N windows, `isOpen`/`.is-open` lighting every card whose window is out. **EMBED MODE** (2026-08-17): the stream's second mounting — `embed-item` (one feed item) renders exactly that postcard and none of the surface (no head box, no fetch — `load()` early-returns with the card pinned — no lens context, and NEITHER nav_state hand: `trackContainer('feed')` and `setHeadY` are gated, or the window's copy would write over the real feed's saved spots on the same route); `.feed-stream-pane.is-embed` neutralizes the well (transparent bed, no feed padding, no indigo side borders); pins still load so the embedded tack tells the truth. The element flyout's post face is the one mounter and the foot chip's own `.is-open` teal accent is what went for good. When it IS set, the CARD wears an **`--indigo-11` border + halo glow** (2026-08-09: ring + bloom box-shadow layered over the contact shadow, hover at half strength, open/expanded at full — reach ≤ ~8.5px, dying inside the 10px card gap). **THE FOOT IS RULED IN FOUR CELLS (2026-08-10, second ask)** — the CAP's arrangement at the card's other end, so the square opens and closes in one grammar: `[refs] │ [chip copy] │ [comments forks] │ [⌃n over ⌄n]`. The strip itself is `align-items: stretch` with NO padding (each cell carries it), which is what lets `.post-square__foot-rule` meet both edges square without the negative-margin trick `__byline-rule` needs; the old row was chip + `q-space` + three tallies at `padding: 5px 9px 7px`. **REFS** at the far-left edge is the flyout press the chip gave up, re-homed and named — `sym_o_developer_guide` (SYMBOLS-prefixed: bare `developer_guide` resolves against Material Icons, finds no ligature and draws an empty 14px box — it did, for one pass), emitting `select`, `.is-on` when this card's panel is open. The **ADDRESS** cell is the only one that gives (`min-width: 0`, so the hash ellipsizes and the three rigid cells never leave the card); its `content_copy` puts the **pathchain address** — `skeletons/<hash>`, not a browser URL — on the clipboard, with the media viewer's glyph-flips-to-`check` receipt keyed on a per-card `copiedId` rather than a shared boolean (thirty cards, one flag, thirty checkmarks). **VOTES** closes at the far right as a COLUMN: `keyboard_arrow_up` over `keyboard_arrow_down` at 9px (tailless, third ask — the tailed 11px pair read as two instructions), each with its own tally; the single `thumb_up` it replaces showed `votes.up` alone, half of what the feed has always answered with. Tallies, not controls — nothing in this strip has ever been a vote button. `.post-square__stat` letters in **Nasalization** now (it was the card's last Space Mono), as does the strip, and `.post-square__foot-act` is `__cap-act` one register smaller (18px). Foot measured 28px. **THE DIVIDER WALK ENDED 2026-08-10** (three asks). The `RgbHairline` was copied to the byline→labels and labels→content seams; the FRIEZE moved UP from byline→labels to close the CAP, replacing the flipped band that had bracketed the foot's (the `--cap` modifier went with it, and `vflip` has no user on this surface); then an ask **re-made the bracket one seam lower, around the LABELS** — a pair `.post-square__hairline--rail` (1px bread instead of 2, the lower one `--flip`ped to indigo-over-cyan so the two indigo ends faced each other across the chips) — **and the day's LAST ask took that pair straight back out**, with both modifiers, leaving `.post-square__hairline` alone at its base weight over the foot. ⚠ **The card reads `cap │ byline │ hairline │ labels │ hairline │ pit │ rgb │ foot` since 2026-08-22** (user ask: "take the cyan-indigo frieze bar from the post cards and incrust it on the top navigation header of the whole screen") — **the motif band left this surface for the window's light-cream top rail** (`media/MediaTabsBar.vue`, `.media-tabs__frieze`), one motif for the whole screen instead of one per card. It stood INSIDE the cap for one earlier pass that morning, as its floor over 3px of padding, before the ask landed on the real target. **The seam it vacated is the cap's own `border-bottom: 2px solid var(--grey-6)`** ("just a thick grey-6 hairline"): the one line on this card at neither the card's weight nor its ink, every other rule running 1px `--grey-5` since the same day's line walk. The cap is a plain flex ROW again — ⚠ if it is ever made a column, the cells need a row box back (`.post-square__cap-rule` rides `align-self: stretch`, which on a column parent stretches the WIDTH).
